Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the affine structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 48. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 277 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_48(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 48. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^277(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the affine structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 803. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 641 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
